Let's design code components in detail.

Helper:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

def _softmax(z, max_exp=50):
    z = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -max_exp, max_exp))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
    return e/s
```
But we often keep z mean zero; max may large; better:
```
def _softmax0(z):
   z=np.asarray(z); z=z-z.max(); e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-30,30)); s=e.sum(); return e/s
```
If z clipped, sum not exactly? OK.

Conv:
```
def _conv(a, n, fs):
    F=rfft(a, fs)
    return irfft(F*F, fs)[:2*n-1]
```
Cross:
```
def _cc(p, a, n, fs2):
    ar = a[::-1]
    c=irfft(rfft(ar, fs2)*rfft(p, fs2), fs2)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Could precompute ar each iter? a changes, so reverse.

  n=job['n']; fs=job['fs']; phase=job['phase']; a0=job['a0']
  z=np.log(a0); z-=z.mean(); z=clip(-max_z,max_z)

  m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; lr=job['lr']; frac=job['frac']; top_frac=job['top_frac']; restart=...

```

if mode == smooth:
   tau = frac*bmax if bmax>0 else 1
   zscore = (b - bmax)/tau
   p = np.exp(np.clip(zscore, -50, 0)); p sum => if zero use top

   K=max(1,int(top_frac*N)); idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]; p=zeros; p[idx]=1/K
# grad:

z -= z.mean()

```

```

Need perhaps optimize with direct simplex projected gradient for local polish. Let's develop that as separate `_polish_simplex(a0, end_time)`.
Project onto simplex algorithm:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(u)+1)) > (css-1))[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    return w
```
Projected gradient for F(a)=max b (sum1). Gradient g=2y (score gradient = 4n y but constant). We can use direction `d = a - alpha * g` then project? Standard coded gradient for indicator: w=Proj(a - η ∇f). Choose η. We can use backtracking/half if improves.
But objective scale: f=max b, gradient ~? For a~1/n, g~1/n? To change a, η ~ n. Since f scale 1/n. If use score F=2n max b, gradient grad=4n y, magnitude O(1). Then step α ~0.01 maybe. Let's use score F=2n*max b. Subgradient of F =4n y_i for active set weights (p sum1). Gradient magnitude ~4n*(1/n)=4? Actually y~∑p a ~1/n; n*y~O(1). Good. So α for projected gradient can be ~0.05-0.5? If we subtract α*grad with grad~1, changes a by 0.1, too large; projection clips. Use α=0.01? Need tune. Compute `eta = 1.0/(np.linalg.norm(grad,2)+1)` maybe. Since grad norm maybe sqrt(n)*? Use Lipschitz unknown. We can line search few alphas: start `eta=0.05/ (grad.max()+eps)`? Now, let's think about correctness of gradient in log-space with active p.
We have a = softmax(z). b_k = ∑_{i} a_i a_{k-i}. For each k: ∂b_k/∂a_i = 2 a_{k-i} if valid. Let y_i =∑_k p_k a_{k-i}. Then ∂(∑ p_k b_k)/∂a_i=2 y_i = 2 g_i? We set g=2*cc. Good.
Smooth objective S=∑p_k b_k with p depends on b? In exact gradient of logsumexp, p depends but gradient of S = ∑p ∂b, not gradient of original if p varies? If p is treated fixed, it's not true gradient of smooth function. But it's valid descent for max as subgradient/smoothing? For logsumexp, gradient is ∑p ∂b with p=softmax(b/τ); p depends, but chain rule yields same (since derivative of logsumexp with p gives ∑p ∂b; no extra terms because p derivative cancels? Check derivative: d L=∑p_k db_k. p_k =e^{b_k/τ}/sum. d p_k = p_k(δb_k/τ -∑pδb/τ). Sum p_k d p_k? Gradient of L = 1/τ ∑ e_i = p? Actually ∂L/∂a = (1/τ)∑ e_k ∂b_k = ∑p ∂b. No extra. So fixed p equal current softmax gives exact gradient of smooth L. Good.
For active uniform p not gradient of scalar function but subgradient; descent allowed.
Gradient in z: a_i=softmax(z). ∂ a_i/∂z_j = a_i(δ_ij - a_j). So ∂/∂z_i = a_i(∑p∂b/∂a_i - ∑_j a_j ∑p∂b/∂a_j) = a_i(2y_i - 2∑a y). We use g=2y; grad_z=a*(g - dot(a,g)). Good.

Normalization z mean zero does not change a, but parameterization has redundancy. We subtract mean after updates to control scale. Gradient is in tangent to z modulo constants; subtracting mean before making z? Actually a only depends on differences; gradient components sum zero? For our grad_z = a*(g - dot(a,g)), sum grad? Not necessarily zero in z? It is gradient on quotient; adding constant to z same. To keep mean zero, we can project grad to zero-mean? The direction can have nonzero sum due to redundancy; but softmax invariant, changing z by sum constant no effect. When we subtract mean after update, fine. However Adam with nonzero-mean component may waste. Better set `grad -= grad.mean()` to remove constant. Previous didn't. But because a weights? Let's check sum grad_z maybe not zero. Let's design `_polish` more concretely.
```
def _polish(a0, end_time, hard_deadline, seed, initial_sigma=0.1):
  n=len(a0); job=make_job; a=norm(a0); best=a; b,score=_score_b(a); rng=default_rng(seed)
  sigma=0.12; stuck=0
  while time < end-0.1:
     # maybe compute current b? We can maintain cur_b, cur_score after accepted.
     r=rng.random()
     candidates=[]
     if r < 0.45:
        # active projected gradient
        if cur_b None: compute
        b=cur_b; bmax=b.max()
        # choose K random between 2 and max(4, int(0.08*N))
        K=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, int(0.08*N)+1)))
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        # weights maybe top sorted? uniform for now
        p=zeros; p[idx]=1/K
        g=2*_cc_from_p(p,a)
        gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))+1e-300
        d=g/gmax
        # maybe center d? Projection handles sum; no need
        for eta in [1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2]:
            w=proj_simplex(a - eta*d)
            s,_=score(w)
            track best candidate
        # also maybe plus gradient? descent only
     elif r<0.65:
        z=log(a); zc=z+rng.normal(0,sigma,n); a_try=softmax(zc)
     elif r<0.8:
        block perturb: choose i,w, add normal to z block
     elif r<0.9:
        mix with best: a_try=0.8*a+0.2*best? normalize? Actually if a current maybe not best; use global job best.
     else:
        frequency: z=log(a)+amp*sin...
     evaluate; if s < best_s - 1e-12: best=a_try; best_s=s; a=a_try; stuck=0; sigma*=0.95
     else stuck++ ; sigma=min(0.6, sigma*1.03); if stuck>80: a=best; sigma=0.2
  return best,best_s
```

def _forward(a, job):
    F = rfft(a, job['fs'])
    b = irfft(F*F, job['fs'])
    b = b[:job['N']]
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if not finite: bmax=0
    if bmax < 0: bmax=0
    score = 2.0*job['n']*bmax
    return F,b,score
```

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

def _as_norm_array(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(32)/32
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   a=np.minimum(a,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
   return a/s
```

`_make_job(n)`:
```
N=2*n-1; fs=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
phase = np.exp(-2j*math.pi*np.arange(fs//2+1)*(n-1)/fs)
return {'n':n,'N':N,'fs':fs,'phase':phase}
```

`_softmax` in optimization:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=z-z.max()
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,0))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```
If z has max not finite, reset. Use max shift, no need mean after.

`_z_from_a(a, max_z)`:
```
a=np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a=a/a.sum()
z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean(); return np.clip(z,-max_z,max_z)
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n):
   a=_as_norm_array(a); m=len(a); y=m*a
   if n==m: return a
   x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   # use linear interp, left/right old boundaries
   y_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,y,left=float(y[0]),right=float(y[-1]))
   y_new=np.maximum(y_new,0)
   s=y_new.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
   return y_new/s  # since y_new sum = new n? Wait y_new density, need sum 1; y_new is density values (sum old=1). Interpolated density approx sum not 1; normalize counts: a=y_new/s_y where s_y=sum y_new.
```
Yes return y_new/sum.

Random modulation:
```
def _modulate(z, rng, amp):
   n=z.size; x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   out=z.copy()
   for _ in range(2):
      f=float(rng.integers(1,5))
      ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi))
      a_=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))*amp
      out += a_ * np.sin(2*math.pi*f*x + ph)
   out-=out.mean(); return np.clip(out,-max_z,max_z)
```
For initial from a: `z=_z_from_a(a); z=_modulate(z,rng,0.3); return _softmax(z)`.

else:
  if v==0 a=ones
  elif v==1 exp(x*beta)
  elif v==2 exp(-(x-beta)^2?) etc
  add modulation
normalize; ensure finite.
```

`_optimize_job(a0, n, end_time, task_end, seed, max_z=18, init_lr=0.25, init_frac=0.5)`:
Let's pseudo:
```
def _optimize_job(a0,n,end_time,seed,task_name='',max_z=18,init_params=None):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    job=_make_job(n)
    a=_as_norm_array(a0)
    if end_time - time <=2: return a,_forward(a,job)[2]
    z=_z_from_a(a,max_z)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); _, b0, best_score=_forward(a,job)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    lr=float(init params); frac=...; alpha=0; trade...
    streak=0; it=0; restart_count=0; noise=0.35
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.2:
       a=_softmax(z)
       F,b,cur=_forward(a,job)
       if not finite cur: a=best_a; z=best_z; cur=best_score; break
       if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=cur; best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); streak=0
       else:
          streak += 1
       if bmax <=0: break # use cur small?
       # p
       tau=max(1e-15, frac*bmax)
       p_soft=_soft(b,bmax,tau)
       # K maybe int(round(active_frac*N)); if alpha>0
       if alpha > 1e-3:
          p_act=_top_uniform(b, K)
          p = alpha*p_act + (1-alpha)*p_soft
          p /= p.sum()
       else: p=p_soft
       y=_cc(F,p,job)
       g=2.0*y
       h_dot=float(np.dot(a,g))
       grad=a*(g-h_dot)
       if n>1: grad -= grad.mean()
       gn=np.abs(grad).max()
       if not finite or gn<1e-15:
           streak += 1
           if streak > 30: restart...
           continue
       grad /= gn
       # Adam
       t+=1
       m=...; v=...; step=...
       z = z - lr*step
       z = z - z.mean()
       if not all finite: z=best_z.copy(); reset...
       z=np.clip(z, -max_z,max_z)
       it+=1
       # anneal
       progress = min(1.0, it/800.0)
       frac = max(min_frac, frac0 * math.exp(-it/frac_scale))
       alpha = min(max_alpha, alpha0 + alpha_ramp*progress)
       active_frac = max(min_active, active0 - active_decay*progress)
       # adapt lr/streak
       if streak > 20: lr = max(0.05, lr*0.95)
       if streak > restart_threshold:
          # restart
          if rng.random()<0.3:
             z = best_z + _modulation_noise(...)
          else:
             z = best_z + rng.normal(0, noise, n)
          z=z-z.mean(); z=clip
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0
          lr=rng.uniform(0.15,0.5)
          frac=rng.uniform(0.3,0.8)
          alpha=rng.uniform(0.0,0.4)
          active_frac=rng.uniform(0.08,0.25)
          streak=0
          if noise < 1.5: noise *= 1.1 else noise=0.5
       # optional if too far from best: if cur > best+0.05 and it_since_best>200: revert
    # finish: one more _softmax best_z and forward
    a=_softmax(best_z); s=...; return a,s
```
Need `bmax` from b: `bmax=float(b.max())`. If bmax <=0, cur maybe 0? But score can't 0. Fallback best.

Now, `_polish` use job and project. Let's write robust project:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,len(v)+1, dtype=float)
    cond = u > (css-1.0)/idx
    if not cond.any(): return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    return w
```
Need v finite.

Polish loop details:
```
def _polish(a0, end_time, hard_deadline, seed):
   n=len(a0); job=_make_job(n)
   a=_as_norm_array(a0); best=a.copy(); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job); best_score=cur
   rng=default_rng(seed); sigma=0.10; stuck=0
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   while time < end_time-0.1:
      if time > hard-0.5 break

         # Need F,b of current a. If b None recompute.
         if F is None or b is None or not hasattr(a,'_same')? We'll just recompute? To avoid staleness, maintain F,b updated whenever a changes. Set F,b,cur after each accepted.
         bmax=b.max(); if bmax>0:

            y=_cc(F,p,job)
            g=2*y; gm=np.max(np.abs(g)); if gm>1e-15:
                d=g/gm; d-=d.mean()
                dmax=np.max(np.abs(d)); if dmax>1e-15: d/=dmax

                if n>1024: etas=[1e-5,1e-4,1e-3]
                for eta in etas:
                    if time > end_time -0.2: break
                    w=_proj_simplex(a - eta*d)
                    _, _, s=_forward(w,job)
                    if s < best_trial_s:
                       best_trial_s=s; best_trial=w
      else:

         elif r<0.88:

         _,_,s=_forward(a_try,job); if s<best_trial_s: best_trial=a_try; best_trial_s=s
      if best_trial is not None and best_trial_s < best_score - 1e-12:
          a=best_trial.copy(); best=a.copy(); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job); best_score=cur; stuck=0; sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.97)
      else:
          stuck +=1
          sigma=min(0.7, sigma*1.08)
          if stuck > 50:
             a=best.copy(); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job); best_score=cur; stuck=0; sigma=0.15
   return best,best_score

```
def _top_uniform(b,K):
  p=np.zeros_like(b)
  if K<=0: K=1
  if K>=b.size: p.fill(1.0/b.size)
  elif K==1: p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
  else:
    part=np.argpartition(b, -K)
    idx=part[-K:]
    if len(idx)<K: idx=np.argsort(b)[-K:]
    p[idx]=1.0/K
  return p
```
`np.argpartition` with -K works if K>0. If K=1, `part[-1]` okay? We can just use.

```
def _soft_weights(b,bmax,tau):
  if tau <= 1e-300: return top1
  u=(b-bmax)/tau
  e=np.exp(np.clip(u,-50,0))
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0 or not finite: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
  else: p=e/s
  return p
```

```
def _optimize_job(a0,n,end_time,seed=0,kind='best',max_z=25,lr0=None,frac0=0.5,alpha0=0.2,noise0=0.35):
    if time.monotonic() >= end_time: return _as_norm_array(a0), math.inf?
    job=_make_job(n); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=_as_norm_array(a0)
    z=_z_from_a(a,max_z)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
    F,b,best_score=_forward(a,job)
    if not finite best_score: best_score=2.0
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if lr0 is None: lr = 0.25*min(1.0, math.sqrt(384.0/max(1,n))) # for n192 factor1, n1920 sqrt0.2=0.447 ->0.112
    frac=frac0; alpha=alpha0; active_frac=0.18; min_frac=0.04; min_active=0.04
    streak=0; no_grad=0; it=0; restarted=0; noise=noise0
    pool=[z.copy()]; last_p_act=None
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.15:
        a=_softmax(z)
        F,b,cur=_forward(a,job)
        if not math.isfinite(cur):
           z=best_z.copy(); a=_softmax(z); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job)
           if not finite cur: break
        if cur < best_score - 1e-13:
           best_score=cur; best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); streak=0
           if len(pool)<6 and it % 25 == 0:
              pool.append(z.copy())
        else:
           streak += 1
        bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        if bmax <= 1e-300:
           break
        # weights
        tau = max(1e-300, frac*bmax)
        p_soft = _soft_weights(b,bmax,tau)
        K = int(round(active_frac*job['N']))
        K = max(1, min(job['N'], K))
        if alpha > 1e-3:
            if last_p_act is None or it % 4 == 0:
                # use clarify if K>1 and n<=? use always
                try: p_act = _clarify_p(b,a,job,K)
                except: p_act = _top_uniform(b,K)
                last_p_act = p_act
            else:
                p_act=last_p_act
            p = alpha*p_act + (1-alpha)*p_soft
            s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=p_soft else p/=s
        else: p=p_soft
        y=_cc(F,p,job)
        g=2.0*y
        hdot=float(np.dot(a,g))
        grad=a*(g-hdot)
        if n>1: grad -= grad.mean()
        gn=float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0
        if (not math.isfinite(gn)) or gn < 1e-15:
            no_grad += 1
            streak += 1
            if no_grad > 20:
                # restart easily
                ...
            continue
        grad /= gn
        t += 1
        b1,b2=0.9,0.999
        m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
        v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(grad*grad)
        mh = m/(1-b1**t)
        vh = v/(1-b2**t)
        upd = mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
        um=float(np.max(np.abs(upd)))
        if math.isfinite(um) and um>0:
            z = z - lr * (upd / um)  # normalize step? Previous normalized upd max to 1. If we normalized grad, upd max maybe 1; dividing by um again makes step lr. Maybe just lr*upd. Let's decide. If grad normalized max1, Adam with betas step max ~1. Use z -= lr*upd. No need um. But if vh weird, clip. Use `if um>...: upd = upd/um` to bound. Then step lr.
```

        z -= z.mean()
        if not all finite: reset z=best_z; reset moments

Code structure:
```
import numpy as np
import time, math
try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft
    def next_fast_len(x):
        x=int(x)
        if x<=1: return 1
        return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
INF=math.inf

def _as_norm_array(a): ...
def _make_job(n): ...
def _forward(a, job): ...
def _cc(F,p,job): ...
def _top_uniform(b,K): ...
def _clarify_p(b,a,job,K): ...
def _soft_weights(b,bmax,tau): ...
def _softmax(z): ...
def _z_from_a(a,max_z=30): ...
def _z_modulate(base,rng,amp,x): ...
def _a_with_mod(a,n,seed,amp=0.25): ...
def _random_smooth(n,seed,parent=None): ...
def _resample(a,n): ...
def _proj_simplex(v): ...
def _score_array(a): ...
def _optimize_job(...): ...
def _polish(...): ...
def propose_candidate(...): ...
```

Need global `height_sequence_1` not redefined. OK.

Let's write helpers carefully.

`_as_norm_array`:
```
def _as_norm_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0:
        return np.ones(16)/16.0
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size)/float(arr.size)
    return arr/s
```

`_make_job`:
```
def _make_job(n):
    n=int(n)
    N=2*n-1
    fs=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    if fs < 2*n-1: fs=next_fast_len(fs*2) # not necessary
    kk=np.arange(fs//2+1, dtype=np.float64)
    phase=np.exp(-2j*math.pi*kk*(n-1)/float(fs))
    return {'n':n,'N':N,'fs':int(fs),'phase':phase}
```

`_forward`:
```
def _forward(a,job):
    n=job['n']; fs=job['fs']
    F=rfft(a, fs)
    b=irfft(F*F, fs)
    b=b[:job['N']]
    # Remove tiny negative
    b=np.where(b>0.0, b, 0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
       return F,b,INF
    bmax=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(bmax) or bmax < 0.0:
        bmax=0.0
    score=2.0*n*bmax/(s*s)
    if not math.isfinite(score): score=INF
    return F,b,score
```
If a not contiguous? fine.

`_cc`:
```
def _cc(F,p,job):
    fs=job['fs']; n=job['n']
    P=rfft(p, fs)
    y=irfft(job['phase']*np.conj(F)*P, fs)
    return y[n-1:2*n-1]
```
`np.conj(F)` each iter. Good.

`_top_uniform`:
```
def _top_uniform(b,K):
    p=np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    N=b.size
    K=int(K)
    if K <=0: K=1
    if K >= N:
        p.fill(1.0/N)
    elif K ==1:
        p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
    else:
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        p[idx]=1.0/float(K)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0:
       p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
    else:
       p/=s
    return p
```
`np.argpartition(b, -K)` with K=0? handled. If K=N? handled. Good.

`_clarify_p`:
```
def _clarify_p(b,a,job,K):
    n=job['n']; N=job['N']
    K=int(K)
    if K<=1:
        return _top_uniform(b,K)
    K=min(K,N)
    # top indices
    if K==N:
       idx=np.arange(N)
    elif K==1:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:].astype(int, copy=False)
    Y=np.zeros((n,K), dtype=np.float64)
    for j,k in enumerate(idx):
        lo=max(0, k-(n-1))
        hi=min(n-1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            # r from k-lo down to k-hi
            Y[lo:hi+1, j] = a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]
    C=Y - Y.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    A=C.T @ C
    scale=np.trace(A)/K if K else 1.0
    reg=1e-4*scale + 1e-12
    A= A + reg*I (I=np.eye(K))
    rhs=reg*np.full(K, 1.0/K)
    try:
       p=np.linalg.solve(A,rhs)
    except:
       p=np.linalg.lstsq(A,rhs,rcond=None)[0]
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-300:
       p=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    else: p/=s
    out=np.zeros(N,dtype=np.float64)
    out[idx]=p
    return out
```
This solves min ||Cp||²+reg||p-uniform||² over active K. If K large, solve cost. For K=1 returns top.
Maybe active top can include constraints not near max but top. Good.

`_soft_weights` as above.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m=z.max()
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z-m,-50.0,0.0))
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(z.size)/float(z.size)
    return e/s
```
If z empty? no.

`_z_from_a`:
```
def _z_from_a(a,max_z=30):
    a=_as_norm_array(a)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    a=a/a.sum()
    z=np.log(a)
    z-=z.mean()
    return np.clip(z,-max_z,max_z)
```

`_z_modulate`:
```
def _z_modulate(base, rng, amp, x, n):
    z=base.copy()
    if amp <=0: return np.clip(z-z.mean(), -30,30)
    for _ in range(2):
       f=float(rng.uniform(0.3, 6.0))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi))
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))*amp
       z = z + c*np.sin(2*math.pi*f*x + ph)
    z -= z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -30, 30)
```

`_a_with_mod`:
```
def _a_with_mod(a,n,seed,amp=0.25):
    a=_resample(a,n)
    z=_z_from_a(a,30)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    z=_z_modulate(z,rng,amp,x,n)
    return _softmax(z)
```

`_random_smooth`:
```
def _random_smooth(n,seed,parent=None):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   v=int(rng.integers(0,9))
   if v==0:
      a=np.ones(n)
   elif v==1:
      a=np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-2,2))*x)
   elif v==2:
      a=np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1,4))*x)
   elif v==3:
      a=np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1,4))*(1-x))
   elif v==4:
      a=x**float(rng.uniform(0.5,3))+1e-3
   elif v==5:
      a=(1-x)**float(rng.uniform(0.5,3))+1e-3
   elif v==6:
      f=float(rng.uniform(0.5,4)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); a=1+0.8*sin(...)
   elif v==7:
      f1=...; a=1+0.7*cos +0.4*sin
   else:
      a=np.abs(rng.normal(0,1,n))+0.3
   if parent is not None and rng.random()<0.25:
       # mix log
       p=_resample(parent,n)
       z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-8)) - np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-8))
       z=z*0.3; a=np.exp(z); # maybe not normalize
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
   a/=a.sum()
   # apply modest modulation
   if rng.random()<0.6:
      a=_a_with_mod(a,n,seed+1,0.2)
   return a
```
Need ensure `_a_with_mod` calls `_resample`, which may use `a` length? It does. In random_smooth, if we call `_a_with_mod` with a same n, _resample returns same, fine.
But mixing parent log weird: `a = exp(z)` then normalize; OK but maybe low. Simpler: if parent and random<0.5, start from parent mod; else above. Let's implement simpler:
```
if parent is not None and rng.random()<0.35:
   return _a_with_mod(parent,n,seed,0.3)
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n):
    a=_as_norm_array(a)
    m=a.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m==n: return a
    y=m*a
    x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    y_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,y,left=float(y[0]),right=float(y[-1]))
    y_new=np.where(np.isfinite(y_new),y_new,0.0)
    y_new=np.maximum(y_new,0.0)
    s=float(y_new.sum())
    if s<1e-300:
       return np.ones(n)/float(n)
    return y_new/s
```
If m=1, interp constant. Good.

`_proj_simplex`:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,v.size+1,dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u * idx > (css - 1.0))  # equivalently u > (css-1)/idx
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(css[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    return w
```
Should be correct.

def _score_array(a):

    job=_make_job(a.size)
    return _forward(a,job)[2]

`_optimize_job` code detailed. Need use global INF.

Let's write optimize carefully:
```
def _optimize_job(a0,n,end_time,seed=0,lr0=None,frac0=0.5,alpha0=0.2,noise0=0.35,max_z=30):
    a=_as_norm_array(a0)
    job=_make_job(n)
    if n <= 0 or time.monotonic() >= end_time:
       _,_,s=_forward(a,job); return a,s
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z=_z_from_a(a,max_z)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
    F,b,best_score=_forward(a,job)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score): best_score=2.0
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    lr_scale = min(1.0, math.sqrt(512.0/max(1,n)))  # n192 1.64 cap1, n1920 sqrt0.266=.516
    if lr0 is None: lr=0.25*lr_scale
    else: lr=lr0*lr_scale
    frac=frac0
    alpha=alpha0
    active_frac=0.18
    min_frac=0.04
    min_active=0.04
    streak=0; no_grad=0; it=0
    noise=noise0
    pool=[z.copy()]
    last_p_act=None
    restart_threshold=120
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.15:
        a=_softmax(z)
        F,b,cur=_forward(a,job)
        if not math.isfinite(cur):
            z=best_z.copy()
            break
        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=cur
            best_z=z.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
            streak=0
            if len(pool)<6 and it % 25 == 0:
                pool.append(z.copy())
            elif len(pool)>=6:
                # replace random non-default? not necessary
                pass
        else:
            streak += 1
        bmax=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        if bmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        tau=max(1e-300, frac*bmax)
        p_soft=_soft_weights(b,bmax,tau)
        K=int(round(active_frac*job['N']))
        K=max(1,min(job['N'],K))
        if alpha > 1e-3:
            if last_p_act is None or it % 4 == 0:
                try:
                    p_act=_clarify_p(b,a,job,K)
                except Exception:
                    p_act=_top_uniform(b,K)
                last_p_act=p_act
            else:
                p_act=last_p_act
            p=alpha*p_act + (1.0-alpha)*p_soft
            ps=float(p.sum())
            if ps > 0 and math.isfinite(ps):
                p=p/ps
            else:
                p=p_soft
        else:
            p=p_soft
        y=_cc(F,p,job)
        g=2.0*y
        hdot=float(np.dot(a,g))
        grad=a*(g-hdot)
        if n > 1:
            grad -= grad.mean()
        gn=float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if (not math.isfinite(gn)) or gn < 1e-15:
            no_grad += 1
            streak += 1
            if no_grad > 30:
                z=best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -max_z, max_z)
                m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0
                lr = 0.25*lr_scale
                no_grad=0
            continue
        grad = grad / gn
        t += 1
        b1=0.9; b2=0.999
        m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
        v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(grad*grad)
        mh = m/(1.0 - b1**t)
        vh = v/(1.0 - b2**t)
        upd = mh/(np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        um=float(np.max(np.abs(upd))) if upd.size else 0.0
        if math.isfinite(um) and um > 1e-300:
            upd = upd / um
            z = z - lr*upd
        z = z - z.mean()
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)
        it += 1
        # anneal
        frac = max(min_frac, frac*0.995)
        alpha = min(0.85, alpha + 0.004)
        active_frac = max(min_active, active_frac - 0.0006)
        if streak > 20:
            lr = max(0.05*lr_scale, lr*0.95)
        if streak > restart_threshold:
            # restart
            if rng.random() < 0.78:
                base = best_z
            else:
                base = pool[int(rng.integers(len(pool)))] if pool else best_z
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                z = _z_modulate(base, rng, noise*1.5, x, n)
            else:
                z = base + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            z = z - z.mean()
            z = np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)
            m.fill(0.0); v.fill(0.0); t=0
            lr = float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.45))*lr_scale
            frac = float(rng.uniform(0.30,0.80))
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.0,0.45))
            active_frac = float(rng.uniform(0.08,0.25))
            streak=0; no_grad=0
            if noise < 1.6:
                noise = min(1.6, noise*1.15)
            else:
                noise = 0.45
            last_p_act = None
        # If extremely far and long no improve, pull back
        if streak > restart_threshold*2 and cur > best_score + 0.02:
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0
            streak=0
            last_p_act=None
    a=_softmax(best_z)
    b,_? We need score:
    _,_,s=_forward(a,job)
    if not math.isfinite(s): s=best_score
    return a,s
```
Concern: In no_grad restart, we don't recenter base if base best_z? We add noise to best_z? In no_grad block: `z=best_z + noise` yes. Need assign `z = best_z + ...` and clip. In code above for no_grad, missing mean? fine.

Now, `_polish` code. Let's write carefully.
```
def _polish(a0,end_time,hard_deadline,seed=0):
    a=_as_norm_array(a0); n=a.size; job=_make_job(n)
    F,b,cur=_forward(a,job)
    if not finite cur: return a,2.0
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    sigma=0.12; stuck=0
    while time.monotonic() < min(end_time, hard_deadline) - 0.2:
       # if time near hard break
       r=rng.random()
       best_trial=None; best_trial_s=INF
       if r < 0.55:
           bmax=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
           if bmax>0:
               N=job['N']
               Kcap=64 if n>1000 else 128
               Kmin=max(1, min(N, int(0.02*N)))
               Kmax=max(Kmin+1, min(N, int(0.12*N), Kcap))
               if Kmax <= Kmin: K=Kmin
               else: K=int(rng.integers(Kmin, Kmax+1))
               try: p=_clarify_p(b,a,job,K)
               except: p=_top_uniform(b,max(1,K))
               g=2.0*_cc(F,p,job)
               gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
               if math.isfinite(gscale) and gscale > 1e-15:
                   d=g/gscale
                   d-=d.mean()
                   dmax=float(np.max(np.abs(d)));
                   if dmax > 1e-15:
                       d /= dmax
                       # etas: max step = mult*gscale
                       mults = (0.05,0.2,1.0,5.0) if n>512 else (0.05,0.2,1.0,5.0,20.0)
                       for mult in mults:
                           if time.monotonic() > end_time - 0.2: break
                           eta=mult*gscale
                           w=_proj_simplex(a - eta*d)
                           _,_,s=_forward(w,job)
                           if s < best_trial_s:
                               best_trial_s=s; best_trial=w
       else:
           z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
           if r < 0.75:
               zc=z + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
           elif r < 0.88:
               i=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-1)))
               w=max(1,min(n, int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//20+1)))))
               i2=min(n,i+w)
               zc=z.copy()
               zc[i:i2] += rng.normal(0, sigma*0.6, i2-i)
           else:
               f=float(rng.uniform(0.3,6)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0))*sigma*2; ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2π))
               zc=z + amp*np.sin(2π*f*x+ph)
           zc=zc - zc.mean()
           a_try=_softmax(zc)
           _,_,s=_forward(a_try,job)
           if s < best_trial_s:
              best_trial_s=s; best_trial=a_try
       if best_trial is not None and best_trial_s < best_score - 1e-12:
           a=best_trial.copy(); best=a.copy(); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job); best_score=cur; stuck=0; sigma=max(0.03, sigma*0.98)
       else:
           stuck +=1
           sigma=min(0.8, sigma*1.08)
           if stuck > 60:
               a=best.copy(); F,b,cur=_forward(a,job); best_score=cur; stuck=0; sigma=0.15
    return best,best_score
```
In gradient branch, if best_trial not improvement, we don't update current a. If best_trial is worse than current but better than best? We only compare to best_score, not current. Suppose current a not best (from previous worse accepted? We always best= current if accepted? In greedy, current a is last improved from best; after rejecting, a remains current. It could be not best? We set a=best on stuck reset; initial a=best. We only update a on improvement, so a is always current but could be worse than best? Actually if improvement, a=best. So a=best always except maybe if we allowed rejects? We don't change a on reject. Good.
Best_trial_s compare to best_score; also need compare to current score? same. OK.
In random branch, if best_trial_s < best_score, accept. This is greedy. Good.
`stuck` increments for each call if no improvement; after 60 reset to best. But a always best; reset no change, just sigma reset. OK.
Should we try current F,b for gradient branch but if a=best (yes) valid. Good.

Polish gradient branch uses `_clarify_p` and projects. This can make many local improvements. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: T=max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    except: T=1300.0
    hard=t0+T-6.0
    seed=int(seed)
    rng_master=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1',None)
       if h is not None:
           p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
           if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
               parent=_as_norm_array(np.maximum(p,0))
    except: parent=None
    if parent is None:
       parent=_random_smooth(192, seed+1)
    best_a=parent.copy()
    best_score=_score_array(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score): best_score=2.0
    def _record(a,s): nonlocal best_a,best_score
       try:
          a=_as_norm_array(a)
       except: return
       if a.size==0: return
       if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_score - 1e-15:
          best_a=a.copy(); best_score=s
```

def _trim(a, tol=1e-10):
  a=np.asarray(a); if a.size==0 return a
  mask = a >= tol
  if not mask.any(): return a
  lo=int(np.argmax(mask)); hi=int(a.size-1 - np.argmax(mask[::-1]))
  b=a[lo:hi+1]
  if b.size == 0: return a
  return b
```

`_trim_ends`:
```
def _trim_ends(a, tol=1e-12):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return a
   mask = a > tol  # not >=
   if not mask.any(): return a
   lo=int(np.argmax(mask))
   hi=int(a.size - 1 - int(np.argmax(mask[::-1])))
   b=a[lo:hi+1]
   return b
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft

    def next_fast_len(x):
        x = int(max(1, x))
        if x <= 1:
            return 1
        return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()

INF = math.inf


def _as_norm_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(16) / 16.0
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _make_job(n):
    n = int(n)
    N = 2 * n - 1
    fs = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    if fs < N:
        fs = next_fast_len(2 * fs)
    kk = np.arange(fs // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    phase = np.exp(-2j * math.pi * kk * (n - 1) / float(fs))
    return {"n": n, "N": N, "fs": int(fs), "phase": phase}


def _forward(a, job):
    n = job["n"]
    fs = job["fs"]
    F = rfft(a, fs)
    b = irfft(F * F, fs)
    b = b[: job["N"]]
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return F, b, INF
    bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(bmax) or bmax < 0.0:
        bmax = 0.0
    score = 2.0 * n * bmax / (s * s)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        score = INF
    return F, b, score


def _cc(F, p, job):
    fs = job["fs"]
    n = job["n"]
    P = rfft(p, fs)
    y = irfft(job["phase"] * np.conj(F) * P, fs)
    return y[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _top_uniform(b, K):
    p = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    N = b.size
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= N:
        p.fill(1.0 / N)
    elif K == 1:
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _kcap(n):
    if n > 1536:
        return 48
    if n > 512:
        return 80
    return 128


def _clarify_p(b, a, job, K):
    n = job["n"]
    N = job["N"]
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
        return _top_uniform(b, max(1, K))
    K = min(K, N)

    if K == N:
        idx = np.arange(N)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:].astype(int, copy=False)

    Y = np.zeros((n, K), dtype=np.float64)
    for j, k in enumerate(idx):
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            Y[lo:hi + 1, j] = a[k - hi: k - lo + 1][::-1]

    C = Y - Y.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    A = C.T @ C
    scale = float(np.trace(A)) / float(K)
    reg = 1e-3 * max(scale, 1e-12) + 1e-10
    A = A + reg * np.eye(K)
    rhs = reg * np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K))
    try:
        p = np.linalg.solve(A, rhs)
    except Exception:
        try:
            p = np.linalg.lstsq(A, rhs, rcond=None)[0]
        except Exception:
            p = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K))

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        p = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K))
    else:
        p /= s

    out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    out[idx] = p
    return out


def _soft_weights(b, bmax, tau):
    if tau <= 1e-300 or bmax <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return p
    u = (b - bmax) / tau
    e = np.exp(np.clip(u, -50.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - m, -50.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _z_from_a(a, max_z=30.0):
    a = _as_norm_array(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = a / max(1e-300, float(a.sum()))
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)


def _z_modulate(base, rng, amp, x, n):
    z = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    if amp <= 1e-12:
        z -= z.mean()
        return np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    for _ in range(2):
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 6.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)) * amp
        z = z + c * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)
    z -= z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)


def _resample(a, n):
    n = int(n)
    a = _as_norm_array(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n)) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a
    y = m * a
    x_old = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, y, left=float(y[0]), right=float(y[-1]))
    y_new = np.where(np.isfinite(y_new), y_new, 0.0)
    y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
    s = float(y_new.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    return y_new / s


def _trim_ends(a, tol=1e-12):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    mask = a > tol
    if not np.any(mask):
        return a
    lo = int(np.argmax(mask))
    hi = int(a.size - 1 - int(np.argmax(mask[::-1])))
    return a[lo: hi + 1]


def _a_with_mod(a, n, seed, amp=0.25):
    a = _resample(a, n)
    z = _z_from_a(a, 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = _z_modulate(z, rng, amp, x, n)
    return _softmax(z)


def _random_smooth(n, seed, parent=None):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if parent is not None and rng.random() < 0.35:
        return _a_with_mod(parent, n, seed + 1, 0.35)

    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    v = int(rng.integers(0, 9))
    if v == 0:
        a = np.ones(n)
    elif v == 1:
        a = np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0)) * x)
    elif v == 2:
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0)) * x)
    elif v == 3:
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0)) * (1.0 - x))
    elif v == 4:
        a = x ** float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0)) + 1e-3
    elif v == 5:
        a = (1.0 - x) ** float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0)) + 1e-3
    elif v == 6:
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        a = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)
    elif v == 7:
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 5.0))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        a = 1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f1 * x + ph1) + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f2 * x + ph2)
    else:
        a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.3

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n)
        s = float(n)
    a = a / s
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        a = _a_with_mod(a, n, seed + 2, 0.25)
    return _as_norm_array(a)


def _proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, v.size + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (css - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (css[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def _score_array(a):
    a = _as_norm_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return INF
    job = _make_job(a.size)
    return _forward(a, job)[2]


def _optimize_job(a0, n, end_time, seed=0, lr0=None, frac0=0.5, alpha0=0.2, noise0=0.35):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = _as_norm_array(a0)
    if a.size != n:
        a = _resample(a, n)
    job = _make_job(n)

    if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
        _, _, s = _forward(a, job)
        return a, s

    max_z = 30.0
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = _z_from_a(a, max_z)
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    _, _, best_score = _forward(a, job)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0

    m = np.zeros(n)
    v = np.zeros(n)
    t = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    lr_scale = min(1.0, math.sqrt(512.0 / float(max(1, n))))
    if lr0 is None:
        lr = 0.25 * lr_scale
    else:
        lr = float(lr0) * lr_scale

    frac = float(frac0)
    alpha = float(alpha0)
    active_frac = 0.18
    min_frac = 0.04
    min_active = 0.04
    streak = 0
    no_grad = 0
    it = 0
    noise = float(noise0)
    pool = [z.copy()]
    last_p_act = None
    restart_threshold = 150 if n < 1024 else 300
    kcap = _kcap(n)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.15:
        a = _softmax(z)
        F, b, cur = _forward(a, job)
        if not math.isfinite(cur):
            z = best_z.copy()
            break

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best_z = z.copy()
            best_a = a.copy()
            streak = 0
            if len(pool) < 6 and it % 25 == 0:
                pool.append(z.copy())
        else:
            streak += 1

        bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        if bmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        tau = max(1e-300, frac * bmax)
        p_soft = _soft_weights(b, bmax, tau)

        K = int(round(active_frac * job["N"]))
        K = max(1, min(job["N"], kcap, K))

        if alpha > 1e-3:
            recompute_period = 2 if n <= 512 else 4
            if last_p_act is None or it % recompute_period == 0:
                try:
                    p_act = _clarify_p(b, a, job, K)
                except Exception:
                    p_act = _top_uniform(b, max(1, K))
                last_p_act = p_act
            else:
                p_act = last_p_act
            p = alpha * p_act + (1.0 - alpha) * p_soft
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps > 0.0 and math.isfinite(ps):
                p = p / ps
            else:
                p = p_soft
        else:
            p = p_soft

        y = _cc(F, p, job)
        g = 2.0 * y
        hdot = float(np.dot(a, g))
        grad = a * (g - hdot)
        if n > 1:
            grad -= grad.mean()

        gn = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if (not math.isfinite(gn)) or gn < 1e-15:
            no_grad += 1
            streak += 1
            if no_grad > 30:
                z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                z -= z.mean()
                z = np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                lr = 0.25 * lr_scale
                no_grad = 0
                last_p_act = None
            continue

        grad = grad / gn
        t += 1
        b1 = 0.9
        b2 = 0.999
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        upd = mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        um = float(np.max(np.abs(upd))) if upd.size else 0.0
        if math.isfinite(um) and um > 1e-300:
            upd = upd / um
            z = z - lr * upd

        z -= z.mean()
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)

        it += 1

        frac = max(min_frac, frac * 0.995)
        alpha = min(0.75, alpha + 0.002)
        active_frac = max(min_active, active_frac - 0.0004)

        if streak > 20:
            lr = max(0.05 * lr_scale, lr * 0.95)

        if streak > restart_threshold:
            if rng.random() < 0.78:
                base = best_z
            else:
                base = pool[int(rng.integers(len(pool)))] if pool else best_z

            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                z = _z_modulate(base, rng, noise * 1.5, x, n)
            else:
                z = base + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)

            z -= z.mean()
            z = np.clip(z, -max_z, max_z)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.45)) * lr_scale
            frac = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.80))
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.45))
            active_frac = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25))
            streak = 0
            no_grad = 0
            if noise < 1.6:
                noise = min(1.6, noise * 1.15)
            else:
                noise = 0.45
            last_p_act = None

        if streak > restart_threshold * 2 and cur > best_score + 0.01:
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            streak = 0
            last_p_act = None

    a = _softmax(best_z)
    _, _, s = _forward(a, job)
    if not math.isfinite(s):
        s = best_score
    return a, s


def _polish(a0, end_time, hard_deadline, seed=0):
    a = _as_norm_array(a0)
    if a.size <= 0:
        return a, INF
    n = a.size
    job = _make_job(n)
    F, b, cur = _forward(a, job)
    if not math.isfinite(cur):
        return a, 2.0

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    sigma = 0.12
    stuck = 0
    kcap = _kcap(n)

    while time.monotonic() < min(end_time, hard_deadline) - 0.2:
        r = rng.random()
        best_trial = None
        best_trial_s = INF

        if r < 0.55:
            bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
            if bmax > 0.0:
                N = job["N"]
                Kmin = max(1, min(N, int(0.02 * N)))
                Kmax = min(kcap, N, max(Kmin + 1, int(0.12 * N)))
                if Kmax <= Kmin:
                    K = Kmin
                else:
                    K = int(rng.integers(Kmin, Kmax + 1))
                try:
                    p = _clarify_p(b, a, job, K)
                except Exception:
                    p = _top_uniform(b, max(1, K))
                g = 2.0 * _cc(F, p, job)
                gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
                if math.isfinite(gscale) and gscale > 1e-15:
                    d = g / gscale
                    d -= d.mean()
                    dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                    if dmax > 1e-15:
                        d /= dmax
                        mults = (0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 5.0) if n > 512 else (0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 5.0, 20.0)
                        for mult in mults:
                            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 0.2:
                                break
                            eta = mult * gscale
                            w = _proj_simplex(a - eta * d)
                            _, _, s = _forward(w, job)
                            if s < best_trial_s:
                                best_trial_s = s
                                best_trial = w
        else:
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
            if r < 0.75:
                zc = z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            elif r < 0.88:
                i = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - 1)))
                w = max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 20 + 1)))))
                i2 = min(n, i + w)
                zc = z.copy()
                zc[i:i2] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma * 0.6, i2 - i)
            else:
                f = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 6.0))
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) * sigma * 2.0
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
                zc = z + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)

            zc -= zc.mean()
            a_try = _softmax(zc)
            _, _, s = _forward(a_try, job)
            if s < best_trial_s:
                best_trial_s = s
                best_trial = a_try

        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_s < best_score - 1e-12:
            a = best_trial.copy()
            best = a.copy()
            F, b, cur = _forward(a, job)
            best_score = cur
            stuck = 0
            sigma = max(0.03, sigma * 0.98)
        else:
            stuck += 1
            sigma = min(0.8, sigma * 1.08)
            if stuck > 60:
                a = best.copy()
                F, b, cur = _forward(a, job)
                best_score = cur
                stuck = 0
                sigma = 0.15

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        T = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        T = 1300.0
    seed = int(seed)

    if T < 100.0:
        final_head = 2.0
    else:
        final_head = 6.0
    hard = t0 + T - final_head
    if hard < t0 + 5.0:
        hard = t0 + 5.0

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                parent = _as_norm_array(np.maximum(p, 0.0))
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = _random_smooth(192, seed + 1)

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_score = _score_array(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0

    def _record(a, s):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        try:
            an = _as_norm_array(a)
        except Exception:
            return
        if an.size == 0:
            return
        cand = an.copy()
        cs = float(s) if math.isfinite(s) else INF
        tr = _trim_ends(cand, 1e-12)
        if tr.size != cand.size:
            try:
                ts = _score_array(tr)
            except Exception:
                ts = INF
            if math.isfinite(ts) and ts < cs:
                cand = tr
                cs = ts
        if math.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_a = cand.copy()
            best_score = float(cs)

    if T < 120.0:
        polish_time = max(5.0, 0.2 * T)
    else:
        polish_time = min(0.25 * T, max(40.0, 0.15 * T))
    polish_start = max(t0 + max(5.0, 0.1 * T), hard - polish_time)
    if polish_start >= hard - 1.0:
        polish_start = hard - 1.0
    opt_budget = max(0.0, polish_start - t0)

    base_n = int(best_a.size)
    max_n = int(min(2048, max(base_n * 10, base_n + 32, 256)))
    sizes = [base_n]
    for mult in (1.7, 2.7, 5.0, 8.0, 10.0):
        s = int(round(base_n * mult / 32.0) * 32)
        if s > base_n and s <= max_n and s not in sizes:
            sizes.append(s)
    if max_n > base_n and max_n not in sizes:
        sizes.append(max_n)
    sizes = sorted(set(sizes))

    scout_time = min(25.0, 0.03 * T, max(0.0, hard - time.monotonic()))
    scout_end = t0 + max(0.0, scout_time)
    trial = 0
    while time.monotonic() < scout_end and trial < 16:
        n = sizes[min(trial // 4, len(sizes) - 1)]
        try:
            a = _random_smooth(n, seed + 100 + trial, parent if parent is not None else best_a)
            s = _score_array(a)
            _record(a, s)
        except Exception:
            pass
        trial += 1

    task_specs = [
        ("base_exact", base_n, 0.16),
        ("best_mod", base_n, 0.13),
        ("random", base_n, 0.13),
    ]
    larger = [s for s in sizes if s > base_n]
    if larger:
        each = 0.58 / float(len(larger))
        for s in larger:
            task_specs.append(("best_mod", s, each))
    else:
        task_specs = [
            ("base_exact", base_n, 0.20),
            ("best_mod", base_n, 0.30),
            ("random", base_n, 0.30),
        ]

    total_w = 0.0
    for _, _, w in task_specs:
        total_w += float(w)
    if total_w <= 0.0:
        total_w = 1.0

    if opt_budget > 30.0:
        for idx, (kind, n, w) in enumerate(task_specs):
            if time.monotonic() >= polish_start - 2.0:
                break
            quota = float(w) / total_w * opt_budget
            if quota < 8.0:
                continue
            slice_end = min(polish_start - 2.0, time.monotonic() + quota)
            if slice_end - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
                continue
            try:
                if kind == "base_exact":
                    a0 = parent if parent is not None else best_a
                    frac0 = 0.35
                    alpha0 = 0.20
                    noise0 = 0.25
                elif kind == "best_mod":
                    a0 = _a_with_mod(best_a, n, seed + 1000 + idx, 0.25)
                    frac0 = 0.55
                    alpha0 = 0.30
                    noise0 = 0.45
                else:
                    a0 = _random_smooth(n, seed + 2000 + idx, parent if parent is not None else best_a)
                    frac0 = 0.70
                    alpha0 = 0.15
                    noise0 = 0.60

                a0 = _as_norm_array(a0)
                if a0.size != n:
                    a0 = _resample(a0, n)
                s0 = _score_array(a0)
                _record(a0, s0)

                a1, s1 = _optimize_job(
                    a0,
                    n,
                    slice_end,
                    seed + 3000 + idx,
                    lr0=None,
                    frac0=frac0,
                    alpha0=alpha0,
                    noise0=noise0,
                )
                _record(a1, s1)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < polish_start - 10.0:
        try:
            end = polish_start - 2.0
            a0 = _a_with_mod(best_a, best_a.size, seed + 777, 0.15)
            a1, s1 = _optimize_job(
                a0,
                best_a.size,
                end,
                seed + 999,
                lr0=None,
                frac0=0.45,
                alpha0=0.35,
                noise0=0.35,
            )
            _record(a1, s1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    polish_end = min(hard - 2.5, t0 + T - 8.0)
    if polish_end < hard - 2.5:
        polish_end = hard - 2.5
    if time.monotonic() < polish_end - 10.0:
        try:
            a1, s1 = _polish(best_a, polish_end, hard, seed + 1234)
            _record(a1, s1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    candidates = []
    try:
        candidates.append(_as_norm_array(best_a))
    except Exception:
        pass
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            candidates.append(_as_norm_array(parent))
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        candidates.append(np.ones(32) / 32.0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    final_cand = [1.0]
    final_score = INF
    for c in candidates:
        try:
            cand = [float(x) for x in _as_norm_array(c)]
            if not cand:
                continue
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
                final_cand = cand
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not final_cand or not all(math.isfinite(float(x)) for x in final_cand):
        final_cand = [1.0]

    return final_cand
```
